> DEVELOPER

Can we start to implement this?

Here's the key idea in the architecture:
The system never modifies the LLM itself — instead it builds a smarter layer around it. When someone asks "why is there high radiation near Eubank and Southern in Albuquerque?", the flow is:

The question gets embedded into a vector
DuckDB checks for semantically similar past questions (using list_cosine_similarity, which DuckDB supports natively)
If there's a high-confidence match with positive user feedback → return the cached answer instantly, no LLM call needed
If no match, the top-k most similar past Q&A pairs get injected into Claude's system prompt as RAG context, along with any curated location knowledge for the area
After the response, the user can thumbs-up/down it
Positively-rated answers enter the cache and also auto-populate the location knowledge store if coordinates are detected

The nice thing for Safecast specifically is the location knowledge store — once someone confirms that the Nuclear Museum explains the readings at that spot, every future question about that area gets that context automatically. Over time you'd build up a geographic knowledge base of "why this area reads high/low" — naturally radioactive geology, nuclear facilities, museums, medical centers, etc.
The Go code plugs directly into the existing web-chat handler. The main additions are the DuckDB tables, an embedding service call, and a /api/feedback endpoint. DuckDB is already in your stack, so no new dependencies there. For embeddings you could start with OpenAI's text-embedding-3-small (very cheap) and later swap in a local model to keep everything self-hosted.

### 1. WEB-CHAT HANDLER - Request/Response Flow

**File:** `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/web-chat/main.go`

#### Request Structure
```go
// Lines 343-354: Chat request format
var chatReq struct {
    Message string              `json:"message"`
    History []anthropicMessage `json:"history,omitempty"`
}
```

#### Claude API Call
**Lines 243-286:** The `callAnthropic()` function makes POST requests to `https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages`:

```go
func callAnthropic(ctx context.Context, apiKey, model string, messages []anthropicMessage, tools []anthropicTool) (*anthropicResponse, error) {
    reqBody := anthropicRequest{
        Model:     model,
        MaxTokens: 4096,
        System:    systemPrompt,
        Messages:  messages,
        Tools:     tools,
    }
    // POST to https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages with x-api-key header
}
```

**Key Details:**
- Model: Defaults to `claude-haiku-4-5-20251001` (line 487)
- Max tokens: 4096 per turn
- System prompt: Detailed Safecast radiation assistant guidance (lines 112-165)
- History truncation: Keeps messages under 150,000 tokens (lines 42-110)

#### Response & Streaming
**Lines 398-466:** Agentic loop handles:
1. Claude response with tool_use blocks
2. MCP tool execution via `/mcp-http` endpoint
3. Tool results fed back to Claude
4. Streaming output as NDJSON chunks (one JSON object per line)

**Streaming Format (Lines 210-239):**
```go
type chunk struct {
    Type  string `json:"type"`    // "text", "error", "done"
    Text  string `json:"text,omitempty"`
    Error string `json:"error,omitempty"`
}
```

**CloudFront Buffering:** Lines 316-339 detect CloudFront and buffer all chunks for batch delivery (CloudFront blocks true chunked streaming).

```go
// mcp_query_log: Tool execution metrics
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS mcp_query_log (
    tool_name VARCHAR,
    params JSON,
    result_count INTEGER,
    duration_ms DOUBLE,
    client_info VARCHAR,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now()
)

// mcp_ai_query_log: AI generation logs
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS mcp_ai_query_log (
    user_id TEXT,
    user_email TEXT,
    session_id TEXT,
    timestamp TIMESTAMP,
    tool_name TEXT,
    generated_query TEXT,
    duration_ms BIGINT,
    commit_hash TEXT,
    error TEXT
)

// chat_questions: Web chat & widget questions + answers
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS chat_questions (
    id BIGINT,
    timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now(),
    question VARCHAR,
    answer VARCHAR,                    // AI response
    source VARCHAR,                    // "web-chat" or "map-widget"
    ip_address VARCHAR,
    user_agent VARCHAR,
    is_mobile BOOLEAN,
    os VARCHAR,
    browser VARCHAR,
    country VARCHAR,
    accept_language VARCHAR,
    referer VARCHAR,
    session_id VARCHAR,
    history_length INTEGER,
    model VARCHAR,
    cloudfront BOOLEAN,
    client_timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ
)
```

#### PostgreSQL Tables (Attached via DuckDB)
**Main Safecast database, queried through DuckDB:**

**File:** `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/pkg/database/database.go` (Lines 1008-1206)

**Key Tables:**

1. **markers** (Lines 1008-1055)
   - Historical radiation measurements from mobile surveys
   - Columns: `doserate` (µSv/h), `date` (epoch), `lat`, `lon`, `altitude`, `detector`, `device_id`, `trackid`, `has_spectrum`
   - Spatial index: `GIST on geom` (POINT geometry)
   - Auto-updates `geom` column from `lat`/`lon` via trigger
   - Constraint: Unique on (doserate, date, lon, lat, countrate, zoom, speed, trackid)

2. **realtime_measurements** (Lines 1060-1075)
   - Real-time sensor data from fixed stations
   - Columns: `device_id`, `value` (CPM or µSv/h), `unit`, `lat`, `lon`, `measured_at` (epoch), `fetched_at`
   - Unique constraint: (device_id, measured_at)

3. **tracks** (Lines 1056-1058)
   - Survey track metadata
   - Column: `trackid` (primary key, TEXT)

4. **spectra** (Lines 1089-1107)
   - Radiological spectrum data
   - Columns: `marker_id` (FK to markers), `channels`, `channel_count` (1024), `energy_min_kev`, `energy_max_kev`, `device_model`, `calibration`, `source_format`, `raw_data` (BYTEA)

5. **uploads** (Lines 1108-1130)
   - Upload metadata
   - Columns: `filename`, `track_id`, `upload_ip`, `recording_date`, `source`, `user_id`, `internal_user_id`, `detector`
   - Indexes on: track_id, created_at, user_id, username

6. **users** (Lines 1133-1153)
   - Authentication
   - Columns: `email` (UNIQUE), `password_hash`, `username` (UNIQUE), `is_admin`, `external_id`, `api_key`

7. **Other tables**: `short_links`, `sessions`, `password_reset_tokens`, `email_verification_tokens`, `translations`

### 5. QUERY PATTERNS: PostgreSQL → DuckDB → Claude

#### Example: sensor_current Tool (Lines 60-191)
**File:** `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/mcp-server/tool_sensor_current.go`

For real-time data lookup:
```go
func sensorCurrentDB(ctx context.Context, deviceID string, minLat, maxLat, minLon, maxLon float64, limit int) {
    // Queries realtime_measurements table
    // Returns latest reading per device via: GROUP BY device_id; MAX(measured_at)
    // Returns as JSON with _ai_hint and _ai_generated_note fields
}
```

**Lines 105-120 (device_id lookup):**
```sql
SELECT id, device_id, device_name, value, unit, 
       to_timestamp(measured_at) AS captured_at, 
       lat, lon, transport
FROM realtime_measurements
WHERE device_id = $1
  AND to_timestamp(measured_at) <= NOW()
ORDER BY measured_at DESC
LIMIT 1
```

**Lines 125-147 (geographic search):**
```sql
SELECT rm.id, rm.device_id, ... 
FROM realtime_measurements rm
INNER JOIN (
    SELECT device_id, MAX(measured_at) as max_measured_at
    FROM realtime_measurements
    WHERE lat >= $1 AND lat <= $2 AND lon >= $3 AND lon <= $4
      AND to_timestamp(measured_at) <= NOW()
    GROUP BY device_id
) latest ON rm.device_id = latest.device_id AND rm.measured_at = latest.max_measured_at
ORDER BY rm.measured_at DESC
LIMIT $5
```

#### Example: query_radiation Tool (Lines 65-172)
**File:** `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/mcp-server/tool_query_radiation.go`

Queries historical mobile survey data from markers table via PostgreSQL (attached to DuckDB):
```sql
WITH top_markers AS (
    SELECT m.id, m.doserate, m.date, m.lat, m.lon, ...
    FROM markers m
    WHERE m.geom && ST_Expand(ST_SetSRID(ST_MakePoint($2, $1), 4326), $3 / 111000.0)
      AND ST_DWithin(m.geom::geography, ST_SetSRID(ST_MakePoint($2, $1), 4326)::geography, $3)
    ORDER BY m.date DESC
    LIMIT $4
)
SELECT m.id, m.doserate AS value, 'µSv/h' AS unit, to_timestamp(m.date) AS captured_at, ...
       ST_Distance(m.geom::geography, ...) AS distance_m,
       u.internal_user_id, usr.username
FROM top_markers m
LEFT JOIN uploads u ON u.track_id = m.trackid
LEFT JOIN users usr ON u.internal_user_id = usr.id::text
ORDER BY m.date DESC
```

**Key optimizations:**
- **Spatial index**: `GIST(geom)` + bbox pre-filter (`&&`) before expensive `ST_DWithin()` distance calc
- **Subquery**: Filters & sorts before joining to uploads/users (limits join cardinality)

### 10. CRITICAL DETAILS FOR DEVELOPERS

**Claude API Integration:**
- Requests include full conversation history (truncated if >150K tokens)
- Tools are passed as JSON schemas converted from MCP tool definitions
- Stop reason = "end_turn" terminates loop; tool_use blocks trigger MCP calls

**Tool Result Truncation:**
- Max result size: 120,000 characters (line 29, web-chat/main.go)
- Prevents single large query from exceeding API token limits

**Database Detection:**
- Tools check `dbAvailable()` before querying PostgreSQL
- Fall back to REST API if DB unavailable (tool_sensor_current.go, line 52-57)

**Real-time vs Historical:**
- `realtime_measurements` = fixed sensors (latest only)
- `markers` = mobile survey track data (historical, with PostGIS indexing)
- System prompt enforces tool selection to avoid confusion

**Chat Logging (unified-server only):**
- Inserts immediately with question → logs after answer received
- Truncates question >5000 chars, answer >50000 chars
- Captures CloudFront headers, user agent parsing, session context

---

This architecture supports low-latency agentic chat with Claude while leveraging a mature PostgreSQL/PostGIS database and modern analytics via DuckDB. No vector search currently; all search is spatial (PostGIS) or keyword-based.

1	// Chat question logging to DuckLake analytics
2	// Captures user questions from the web-chat and map widget with request metadata
3

5	
6	import (
7		"log"
8		"net"
9		"net/http"

11		"time"
12	)

14	// logChatQuestion logs a user's chat question to DuckLake and returns a unique row ID.
15	// The returned ID can be passed to logChatAnswer to attach the AI response.
16	func logChatQuestion(r *http.Request, question, source, model, sessionID string, historyLen int, clientTimestamp string) int64 {
17		if !duckDBAvailable() {
18			return 0
19		}
20	
21		if len(question) > 5000 {
22			question = question[:5000]
23		}
24	
25		ip := getClientIP(r)
26		ua := r.Header.Get("User-Agent")
27		isMobile, osName, browser := parseUserAgent(ua)
28		country := r.Header.Get("CloudFront-Viewer-Country")
29		acceptLang := r.Header.Get("Accept-Language")
30		if len(acceptLang) > 200 {
31			acceptLang = acceptLang[:200]
32		}
33		referer := r.Header.Get("Referer")
34		isCloudFront := r.Header.Get("CloudFront-Viewer-Country") != "" ||
35			r.Header.Get("CloudFront-Forwarded-Proto") != "" ||
36			r.Header.Get("X-Amz-Cf-Id") != ""
37	
38		// Parse client timestamp; use nil if not provided or invalid
39		var clientTS interface{}
40		if clientTimestamp != "" {
41			clientTS = clientTimestamp
42		}
43	
44		// Generate unique ID (DuckLake doesn't support RETURNING or sequences)
45		id := time.Now().UnixNano()
46	
47		_, err := duckDB.Exec(`
48			INSERT INTO chat_questions (
49				id, question, source, ip_address, user_agent, is_mobile,
50				os, browser, country, accept_language, referer,
51				session_id, history_length, model, cloudfront, client_timestamp
52			) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
53			id, question, source, ip, ua, isMobile,
54			osName, browser, country, acceptLang, referer,
55			sessionID, historyLen, model, isCloudFront, clientTS,
56		)
57		if err != nil {
58			log.Printf("chat_questions insert error: %v", err)
59			return 0
60		}
61		return id
62	}
63

98		_, err := duckDB.Exec(`
99			INSERT INTO chat_questions (
100				id, question, answer, source, ip_address, user_agent, is_mobile,
101				os, browser, country, accept_language, referer,
102				session_id, history_length, model, cloudfront, client_timestamp
103			) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
104			id, question, answer, source, ip, ua, isMobile,
105			osName, browser, country, acceptLang, referer,
106			sessionID, historyLen, model, isCloudFront, clientTS,
107		)
108		if err != nil {
109			log.Printf("chat_questions insert (with answer) error: %v", err)
110		}
111	}

113	// getClientIP extracts the client IP from the request, respecting proxy headers.
114	func getClientIP(r *http.Request) string {
115		// X-Forwarded-For may contain multiple IPs: client, proxy1, proxy2
116		if xff := r.Header.Get("X-Forwarded-For"); xff != "" {
117			parts := strings.SplitN(xff, ",", 2)
118			ip := strings.TrimSpace(parts[0])
119			if ip != "" {
120				return ip
121			}
122		}
123		if xri := r.Header.Get("X-Real-IP"); xri != "" {
124			return strings.TrimSpace(xri)
125		}
126		host, _, err := net.SplitHostPort(r.RemoteAddr)
127		if err != nil {
128			return r.RemoteAddr
129		}
130		return host
131	}
132	
133	// parseUserAgent extracts mobile/desktop, OS, and browser from User-Agent string.
134	func parseUserAgent(ua string) (isMobile bool, osName, browser string) {
135		lower := strings.ToLower(ua)
136	
137		// Mobile detection
138		isMobile = strings.Contains(lower, "mobile") ||
139			strings.Contains(lower, "android") && !strings.Contains(lower, "tablet") ||
140			strings.Contains(lower, "iphone") ||
141			strings.Contains(lower, "ipod")
142	
143		// OS detection
144		switch {
145		case strings.Contains(lower, "iphone") || strings.Contains(lower, "ipad") || strings.Contains(lower, "ipod"):
146			osName = "iOS"
147		case strings.Contains(lower, "android"):
148			osName = "Android"
149		case strings.Contains(lower, "windows"):
150			osName = "Windows"
151		case strings.Contains(lower, "macintosh") || strings.Contains(lower, "mac os"):
152			osName = "macOS"
153		case strings.Contains(lower, "linux"):
154			osName = "Linux"
155		case strings.Contains(lower, "cros"):
156			osName = "ChromeOS"
157		default:
158			osName = "Unknown"
159		}
160	
161		// Browser detection (order matters — check specific before generic)
162		switch {
163		case strings.Contains(lower, "edg/") || strings.Contains(lower, "edge/"):
164			browser = "Edge"
165		case strings.Contains(lower, "opr/") || strings.Contains(lower, "opera"):
166			browser = "Opera"
167		case strings.Contains(lower, "firefox/"):
168			browser = "Firefox"
169		case strings.Contains(lower, "chrome/") && !strings.Contains(lower, "chromium"):
170			browser = "Chrome"
171		case strings.Contains(lower, "safari/") && !strings.Contains(lower, "chrome"):
172			browser = "Safari"
173		case strings.Contains(lower, "chromium"):
174			browser = "Chromium"
175		default:
176			browser = "Unknown"
177		}
178	
179		return
180	}

105	func writeChunkBuffered(w http.ResponseWriter, c chunk, buffer *[]chunk, useBuffer bool) {
106		if useBuffer {
107			*buffer = append(*buffer, c)
108		} else {
109			writeChunk(w, c)
110		}
111	}
112	
113	func flushBuffer(w http.ResponseWriter, buffer []chunk) {
114		for _, c := range buffer {
115			writeChunk(w, c)
116		}
117	}
118	
119	func callAnthropic(ctx context.Context, apiKey, model, systemPrompt string, messages []anthropicMessage, tools []anthropicTool) (*anthropicResponse, error) {
120		reqBody := anthropicRequest{
121			Model:     model,
122			MaxTokens: 4096,
123			System:    systemPrompt,
124			Messages:  messages,
125			Tools:     tools,
126		}
127		body, err := json.Marshal(reqBody)
128		if err != nil {
129			return nil, err
130		}
131	
132		req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, "https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages", bytes.NewReader(body))
133		if err != nil {
134			return nil, err
135		}
136		req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
137		req.Header.Set("x-api-key", apiKey)
138		req.Header.Set("anthropic-version", "2023-06-01")
139	
140		resp, err := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
141		if err != nil {
142			return nil, err
143		}
144		defer resp.Body.Close()
145	
146		raw, err := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
147		if err != nil {
148			return nil, err
149		}
150	
151		var ar anthropicResponse
152		if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &ar); err != nil {
153			return nil, fmt.Errorf("parse response: %w", err)
154		}
155		if ar.Error != nil {
156			return nil, fmt.Errorf("anthropic %s: %s", ar.Error.Type, ar.Error.Message)
157		}
158		return &ar, nil
159	}
160	
161	func mcpToolsToAnthropic(tools []mcp.Tool) []anthropicTool {
162		var out []anthropicTool
163		for _, t := range tools {
164			schema, _ := json.Marshal(t.InputSchema)
165			out = append(out, anthropicTool{
166				Name:        t.Name,
167				Description: t.Description,
168				InputSchema: json.RawMessage(schema),
169			})
170		}
171		return out
172	}

174	func handleWebChat(mcpURL, apiKey, model string) http.HandlerFunc {
175		return func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
176			w.Header().Set("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*")
177			w.Header().Set("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "POST, OPTIONS")
178			w.Header().Set("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "Content-Type")
179			if r.Method == http.MethodOptions {
180				w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
181				return
182			}
183	
184			isCloudFront := r.Header.Get("CloudFront-Viewer-Country") != "" ||
185				r.Header.Get("CloudFront-Forwarded-Proto") != "" ||
186				r.Header.Get("X-Amz-Cf-Id") != ""
187	
188			w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/x-ndjson")
189			if !isCloudFront {
190				w.Header().Set("Transfer-Encoding", "chunked")
191				w.Header().Set("X-Accel-Buffering", "no")
192			}
193			w.Header().Set("Cache-Control", "no-cache, no-store")
194	
195			var buffer []chunk
196			ctx := r.Context()
197	
198			var chatReq struct {
199				Message         string             `json:"message"`
200				History         []anthropicMessage `json:"history,omitempty"`
201				Source          string             `json:"source,omitempty"`
202				Lang            string             `json:"lang,omitempty"`
203				ClientTimestamp string             `json:"client_timestamp,omitempty"`
204			}
205			if err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&chatReq); err != nil || chatReq.Message == "" {
206				w.WriteHeader(http.StatusBadRequest)
207				writeChunkBuffered(w, chunk{Type: "error", Error: "invalid request: message required"}, &buffer, isCloudFront)
208				if isCloudFront {
209					flushBuffer(w, buffer)
210				}
211				return
212			}
213	
214			// Default source if not provided by frontend
215			source := chatReq.Source
216			if source == "" {
217				source = "web-chat"
218			}
219			// Capture request metadata now; the full row (with answer) is inserted after the AI responds
220			chatReqRef := r
221			chatQuestion := chatReq.Message
222			chatSource := source
223			chatModel := model
224			chatHistory := len(chatReq.History)
225			chatClientTS := chatReq.ClientTimestamp
226			var answerText strings.Builder
227	
228			mc, err := mcpclient.NewStreamableHttpClient(mcpURL)
229			if err != nil {
230				writeChunkBuffered(w, chunk{Type: "error", Error: fmt.Sprintf("MCP connect: %v", err)}, &buffer, isCloudFront)
231				if isCloudFront {
232					flushBuffer(w, buffer)
233				}
234				return
235			}
236			defer mc.Close()
237	
238			if _, err := mc.Initialize(ctx, mcp.InitializeRequest{
239				Params: mcp.InitializeParams{
240					ProtocolVersion: mcp.LATEST_PROTOCOL_VERSION,
241					ClientInfo:      mcp.Implementation{Name: "safecast-web-chat", Version: "1.0.0"},
242				},
243			}); err != nil {
244				writeChunkBuffered(w, chunk{Type: "error", Error: fmt.Sprintf("MCP init: %v", err)}, &buffer, isCloudFront)
245				if isCloudFront {
246					flushBuffer(w, buffer)
247				}
248				return
249			}
250	
251			toolsResult, err := mc.ListTools(ctx, mcp.ListToolsRequest{})
252			if err != nil {
253				writeChunkBuffered(w, chunk{Type: "error", Error: fmt.Sprintf("list tools: %v", err)}, &buffer, isCloudFront)
254				if isCloudFront {
255					flushBuffer(w, buffer)
256				}
257				return
258			}
259			tools := mcpToolsToAnthropic(toolsResult.Tools)
260	
261			messages := chatReq.History
262			if messages == nil {
263				messages = []anthropicMessage{}
264			}
265			messages = append(messages, anthropicMessage{Role: "user", Content: chatReq.Message})
266	
267			sysPrompt := webChatSystemPromptForLang(chatReq.Lang)
268	
269			for {
270				resp, err := callAnthropic(ctx, apiKey, model, sysPrompt, messages, tools)
271				if err != nil {
272					writeChunkBuffered(w, chunk{Type: "error", Error: err.Error()}, &buffer, isCloudFront)
273					if isCloudFront {
274						flushBuffer(w, buffer)
275					}
276					return
277				}
278	
279				messages = append(messages, anthropicMessage{
280					Role:    "assistant",
281					Content: resp.Content,
282				})
283	
284				var toolUses []contentBlock
285				for _, block := range resp.Content {
286					switch block.Type {
287					case "text":
288						answerText.WriteString(block.Text)
289						writeChunkBuffered(w, chunk{Type: "text", Text: block.Text}, &buffer, isCloudFront)
290					case "tool_use":
291						toolUses = append(toolUses, block)
292					}
293				}
294	
295				if resp.StopReason == "end_turn" || len(toolUses) == 0 {
296					break
297				}
298	
299				var toolResults []contentBlock
300				for _, tu := range toolUses {
301					var args map[string]any
302					_ = json.Unmarshal(tu.Input, &args)
303	
304					callReq := mcp.CallToolRequest{}
305					callReq.Params.Name = tu.Name
306					callReq.Params.Arguments = args
307	
308					var resultText string
309					toolResult, err := mc.CallTool(ctx, callReq)
310					if err != nil {
311						resultText = fmt.Sprintf("tool error: %v", err)
312					} else {
313						for _, c := range toolResult.Content {
314							if tc, ok := c.(mcp.TextContent); ok {
315								resultText += tc.Text
316							}
317						}
318					}
319	
320					toolResults = append(toolResults, contentBlock{
321						Type:      "tool_result",
322						ToolUseID: tu.ID,
323						Content:   resultText,
324					})
325				}
326	
327				messages = append(messages, anthropicMessage{
328					Role:    "user",
329					Content: toolResults,
330				})
331			}
332	
333			logChatQuestionWithAnswer(chatReqRef, chatQuestion, chatSource, chatModel, "", chatHistory, chatClientTS, strings.TrimSpace(answerText.String()))
334	
335			writeChunkBuffered(w, chunk{Type: "done"}, &buffer, isCloudFront)
336			if isCloudFront {
337				flushBuffer(w, buffer)
338			}
339		}
340	}
341	
342	// RegisterMCP starts the MCP server on a separate port (default 3333).
343	func RegisterMCP() {
344		log.Println("DEBUG: safecast unified server with MCP integration")
345	
346		// Initialize DuckDB for analytics
347		if err := initDuckDBAnalytics(); err != nil {
348			log.Printf("Warning: DuckDB initialization failed: %v (analytics features disabled)", err)
349		}
350	
351		// Initialize hints loader
352		hintsDir := os.Getenv("MCP_HINTS_DIR")
353		if hintsDir == "" {
354			execPath, _ := os.Executable()
355			hintsDir = filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(execPath), "hints")
356		}
357	
358		mcpHintsLoader = modeladapter.NewHintsLoader(hintsDir)
359		if err := mcpHintsLoader.Load(); err != nil {
360			log.Printf("Warning: failed to load hints: %v (using default hints)", err)
361		} else {
362			log.Printf("Loaded hints for models: %v", mcpHintsLoader.GetAllModels())
363		}
364	
365		mcpModelAdapter = modeladapter.NewAdapter()
366		mcpModelAdapter.SetHintsLoader(mcpHintsLoader)
367	
368		// Create MCP server
369		mcpServer := server.NewMCPServer("safecast-mcp", "1.0.0")
370	
371		if db != nil {
372			log.Println("Using existing PostgreSQL connection for MCP")
373		}
374		if duckDB != nil {
375			log.Println("Using existing DuckDB connection for MCP analytics")
376		}
377	
378		// Register MCP tools
379		mcpServer.AddTool(
380			mcp.NewTool("ping", mcp.WithDescription("Health check tool")),
381			instrumentMCP("ping", func(ctx context.Context, req mcp.CallToolRequest) (*mcp.CallToolResult, error) {
382				return mcp.NewToolResultText("pong"), nil
383			}),
384		)
385	
386		mcpServer.AddTool(queryRadiationToolDef, instrumentMCP("query_radiation", handleQueryRadiation))
387		mcpServer.AddTool(searchAreaToolDef, instrumentMCP("search_area", handleSearchArea))
388		mcpServer.AddTool(listTracksToolDef, instrumentMCP("list_tracks", handleListTracks))
389		mcpServer.AddTool(getTrackToolDef, instrumentMCP("get_track", handleGetTrack))
390		mcpServer.AddTool(deviceHistoryToolDef, instrumentMCP("device_history", handleDeviceHistory))
391		mcpServer.AddTool(getSpectrumToolDef, instrumentMCP("get_spectrum", handleGetSpectrum))
392		mcpServer.AddTool(listSpectraToolDef, instrumentMCP("list_spectra", handleListSpectra))
393		mcpServer.AddTool(radiationInfoToolDef, instrumentMCP("radiation_info", handleRadiationInfo))
394		mcpServer.AddTool(dbInfoToolDef, instrumentMCP("db_info", handleDBInfo))
395		mcpServer.AddTool(listSensorsToolDef, instrumentMCP("list_sensors", handleListSensors))
396		mcpServer.AddTool(sensorCurrentToolDef, instrumentMCP("sensor_current", handleSensorCurrent))
397		mcpServer.AddTool(sensorHistoryToolDef, instrumentMCP("sensor_history", handleSensorHistory))
398		mcpServer.AddTool(queryAnalyticsToolDef, instrumentMCP("query_analytics", handleQueryAnalytics))
399		mcpServer.AddTool(radiationStatsToolDef, instrumentMCP("radiation_stats", handleRadiationStats))

1	package main
2	
3	import (
4		"database/sql"
5		"encoding/json"
6		"fmt"
7		"log"
8		"os"
9		"time"
10	
11		_ "github.com/duckdb/duckdb-go/v2"
12	)
13	
14	var duckDB *sql.DB
15	
16	func initDuckDB() error {
17		// Open in-memory DuckDB — all persistent data lives in DuckLake (PostgreSQL + Parquet)
18		var err error
19		duckDB, err = sql.Open("duckdb", "")
20		if err != nil {
21			return fmt.Errorf("failed to open duckdb: %w", err)
22		}
23	
24		duckDB.SetMaxOpenConns(1)
25		duckDB.SetMaxIdleConns(1)
26		duckDB.SetConnMaxLifetime(0)
27	
28		if err := duckDB.Ping(); err != nil {
29			return fmt.Errorf("failed to ping duckdb: %w", err)
30		}
31	
32		log.Println("DuckDB initialized (in-memory)")
33	
34		// Install and load required extensions
35		for _, ext := range []string{"ducklake", "postgres"} {
36			if _, err := duckDB.Exec(fmt.Sprintf("INSTALL %s;", ext)); err != nil {
37				log.Printf("Warning: INSTALL %s failed: %v", ext, err)
38			}
39			if _, err := duckDB.Exec(fmt.Sprintf("LOAD %s;", ext)); err != nil {
40				return fmt.Errorf("LOAD %s: %w", ext, err)
41			}
42		}
43	
44		// Attach DuckLake catalog via PostgreSQL
45		ducklakePGURL := os.Getenv("DUCKLAKE_PG_URL")
46		if ducklakePGURL == "" {
47			ducklakePGURL = "dbname=ducklake_catalog host=localhost user=ducklake_rw"
48		}
49		dataPath := os.Getenv("DUCKLAKE_DATA_PATH")
50		if dataPath == "" {
51			dataPath = "/var/lib/safecast/ducklake/"
52		}
53	
54		attachQuery := fmt.Sprintf(
55			"ATTACH 'ducklake:postgres:%s' AS analytics (DATA_PATH '%s');",
56			ducklakePGURL, dataPath,
57		)
58		if _, err := duckDB.Exec(attachQuery); err != nil {
59			return fmt.Errorf("attach DuckLake: %w", err)
60		}
61		log.Printf("DuckLake attached (catalog=PostgreSQL, data=%s)", dataPath)
62	
63		// Use analytics as default database
64		if _, err := duckDB.Exec("USE analytics;"); err != nil {
65			return fmt.Errorf("USE analytics: %w", err)
66		}
67	
68		// Also attach main Safecast PostgreSQL for cross-database queries (read-only)
69		pgURL := os.Getenv("DATABASE_URL")
70		if pgURL != "" {
71			query := fmt.Sprintf(
72				"ATTACH '%s' AS postgres_db (TYPE POSTGRES, READ_ONLY)",
73				pgURL,
74			)
75			if _, err := duckDB.Exec(query); err != nil {
76				log.Printf("Warning: failed to attach postgres: %v", err)
77			} else {
78				log.Println("Safecast PostgreSQL attached as postgres_db")
79			}
80		}
81	
82		// Create analytics schema in DuckLake
83		// DuckLake doesn't support multi-statement exec, so run each separately
84		tables := []string{
85			`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS mcp_query_log (
86				tool_name VARCHAR,
87				params JSON,
88				result_count INTEGER,
89				duration_ms DOUBLE,
90				client_info VARCHAR,
91				created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now()
92			)`,
93			`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS mcp_ai_query_log (
94				user_id TEXT,
95				user_email TEXT,
96				session_id TEXT,
97				timestamp TIMESTAMP,
98				tool_name TEXT,
99				generated_query TEXT,
100				duration_ms BIGINT,
101				commit_hash TEXT,
102				error TEXT
103			)`,
104			`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS chat_questions (
105				id BIGINT,
106				timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now(),
107				question VARCHAR,
108				source VARCHAR,
109				ip_address VARCHAR,
110				user_agent VARCHAR,
111				is_mobile BOOLEAN,
112				os VARCHAR,
113				browser VARCHAR,
114				country VARCHAR,
115				accept_language VARCHAR,
116				referer VARCHAR,
117				session_id VARCHAR,
118				history_length INTEGER,
119				model VARCHAR,
120				cloudfront BOOLEAN,
121				client_timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ,
122				answer VARCHAR
123			)`,
124		}
125	
126		for _, ddl := range tables {
127			if _, err := duckDB.Exec(ddl); err != nil {
128				log.Printf("Warning: DuckLake DDL failed: %v (table may already exist)", err)
129			}
130		}
131	
132		log.Println("DuckLake analytics schema ready")
133		return nil
134	}
135	
136	// LogQueryAsync logs a tool execution to DuckLake asynchronously.
137	func LogQueryAsync(toolName string, params map[string]any, resultCount int, duration time.Duration, clientInfo string) {
138		if duckDB == nil {
139			return
140		}
141	
142		go func() {
143			paramsJSON, err := json.Marshal(params)
144			if err != nil {
145				log.Printf("Error marshaling params to JSON: %v", err)
146				return
147			}
148			paramsStr := string(paramsJSON)
149	
150			_, execErr := duckDB.Exec(`
151				INSERT INTO mcp_query_log (tool_name, params, result_count, duration_ms, client_info)
152				VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
153			`, toolName, paramsStr, resultCount, float64(duration.Milliseconds()), clientInfo)
154	
155			if execErr != nil {
156				log.Printf("Error logging query to DuckLake: %v", execErr)
157			}
158		}()
159	}
160	
161	// Analytics Functions
162	
163	// GetToolUsageStats returns usage statistics for tools.
164	func GetToolUsageStats() ([]map[string]any, error) {
165		if duckDB == nil {
166			return nil, fmt.Errorf("duckdb not initialized")
167		}
168	
169		rows, err := duckDB.Query(`
170			SELECT tool_name, COUNT(*) as calls, AVG(duration_ms) as avg_duration, MAX(duration_ms) as max_duration
171			FROM mcp_query_log
172			GROUP BY tool_name
173			ORDER BY calls DESC
174		`)
175		if err != nil {
176			return nil, err
177		}
178		defer rows.Close()
179	
180		var stats []map[string]any
181		for rows.Next() {
182			var toolName string
183			var calls int64
184			var avgDur, maxDur float64
185			if err := rows.Scan(&toolName, &calls, &avgDur, &maxDur); err != nil {
186				return nil, err
187			}
188			stats = append(stats, map[string]any{
189				"tool":   toolName,
190				"calls":  calls,
191				"avg_ms": avgDur,
192				"max_ms": maxDur,
193			})
194		}
195		return stats, nil
196	}
197	
198	// QueryPostgresAnalytics executes an analytical query on the attached Postgres DB.
199	func QueryPostgresAnalytics(query string, args ...any) ([]map[string]any, error) {
200		if duckDB == nil {
201			return nil, fmt.Errorf("duckdb not initialized")
202		}
203	
204		rows, err := duckDB.Query(query, args...)
205		if err != nil {
206			return nil, err
207		}
208		defer rows.Close()
209	
210		cols, err := rows.Columns()
211		if err != nil {
212			return nil, err
213		}
214	
215		var results []map[string]any
216		for rows.Next() {
217			columns := make([]interface{}, len(cols))
218			columnPointers := make([]interface{}, len(cols))
219			for i := range columns {
220				columnPointers[i] = &columns[i]
221			}
222	
223			if err := rows.Scan(columnPointers...); err != nil {
224				return nil, err
225			}
226	
227			row := make(map[string]any)
228			for i, colName := range cols {
229				val := columns[i]
230				if b, ok := val.([]byte); ok {
231					row[colName] = string(b)
232				} else {
233					row[colName] = val
234				}
235			}
236			results = append(results, row)
237		}
238		return results, nil
239	}
240

1	// DuckDB Analytics Initialization for Unified Server
2	// Uses DuckLake with PostgreSQL catalog for shared analytics across all services
3	
4	package main
5	
6	import (
7		"database/sql"
8		"fmt"
9		"log"
10		"os"
11		"strings"
12	
13		_ "github.com/duckdb/duckdb-go/v2"
14	)
15	
16	var duckDB *sql.DB
17	
18	// initDuckDBAnalytics initializes DuckDB with DuckLake catalog backed by PostgreSQL.
19	// This allows multiple services to share the same analytics tables concurrently.
20	func initDuckDBAnalytics() error {
21		// Open in-memory DuckDB — all persistent data lives in DuckLake (PostgreSQL + Parquet)
22		var err error
23		duckDB, err = sql.Open("duckdb", "")
24		if err != nil {
25			return fmt.Errorf("failed to open duckdb: %w", err)
26		}
27	
28		duckDB.SetMaxOpenConns(1)
29		duckDB.SetMaxIdleConns(1)
30		duckDB.SetConnMaxLifetime(0)
31	
32		if err := duckDB.Ping(); err != nil {
33			return fmt.Errorf("failed to ping duckdb: %w", err)
34		}
35	
36		log.Println("DuckDB initialized (in-memory)")
37	
38		// Install and load required extensions
39		for _, ext := range []string{"ducklake", "postgres"} {
40			if _, err := duckDB.Exec(fmt.Sprintf("INSTALL %s;", ext)); err != nil {
41				log.Printf("Warning: INSTALL %s failed: %v", ext, err)
42			}
43			if _, err := duckDB.Exec(fmt.Sprintf("LOAD %s;", ext)); err != nil {
44				return fmt.Errorf("LOAD %s: %w", ext, err)
45			}
46		}
47	
48		// Attach DuckLake catalog via PostgreSQL
49		ducklakePGURL := os.Getenv("DUCKLAKE_PG_URL")
50		if ducklakePGURL == "" {
51			ducklakePGURL = "dbname=ducklake_catalog host=localhost user=ducklake_rw"
52		}
53		dataPath := os.Getenv("DUCKLAKE_DATA_PATH")
54		if dataPath == "" {
55			dataPath = "/var/lib/safecast/ducklake/"
56		}
57	
58		attachQuery := fmt.Sprintf(
59			"ATTACH 'ducklake:postgres:%s' AS analytics (DATA_PATH '%s');",
60			ducklakePGURL, dataPath,
61		)
62		if _, err := duckDB.Exec(attachQuery); err != nil {
63			return fmt.Errorf("attach DuckLake: %w", err)
64		}
65		log.Printf("DuckLake attached (catalog=PostgreSQL, data=%s)", dataPath)
66	
67		// Use analytics as default database
68		if _, err := duckDB.Exec("USE analytics;"); err != nil {
69			return fmt.Errorf("USE analytics: %w", err)
70		}
71	
72		// Also attach main Safecast PostgreSQL for cross-database queries (read-only)
73		databaseURL := os.Getenv("DATABASE_URL")
74		if databaseURL == "" {
75			// Unified server uses -db-conn flag; construct URL from that
76			// Fall back to environment variable
77		}
78		if databaseURL != "" {
79			if err := attachPostgres(databaseURL); err != nil {
80				log.Printf("Warning: PostgreSQL attach failed: %v (cross-db analytics disabled)", err)
81			} else {
82				log.Println("Safecast PostgreSQL attached for cross-database queries")
83			}
84		}
85	
86		// Create analytics schema in DuckLake
87		if err := createDuckDBSchema(); err != nil {
88			log.Printf("Warning: failed to create DuckLake schema: %v", err)
89		}
90	
91		return nil
92	}
93	
94	// attachPostgres attaches the main Safecast PostgreSQL for read-only cross-database queries
95	func attachPostgres(databaseURL string) error {
96		attachStr := databaseURL
97		if !strings.Contains(databaseURL, "?") {
98			attachStr = databaseURL + "?sslmode=prefer"
99		}
100	
101		query := fmt.Sprintf("ATTACH '%s' AS postgres_db (TYPE POSTGRES, READ_ONLY);", attachStr)
102		if _, err := duckDB.Exec(query); err != nil {
103			return fmt.Errorf("attach postgres: %w", err)
104		}
105		return nil
106	}
107	
108	// createDuckDBSchema creates the shared analytics tables in DuckLake
109	func createDuckDBSchema() error {
110		// DuckLake doesn't support multi-statement exec, so run each separately
111		tables := []string{
112			`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS mcp_query_log (
113				tool_name VARCHAR,
114				params JSON,
115				result_count INTEGER,
116				duration_ms DOUBLE,
117				client_info VARCHAR,
118				created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now()
119			)`,
120			`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS mcp_ai_query_log (
121				user_id VARCHAR,
122				user_email VARCHAR,
123				session_id VARCHAR,
124				timestamp TIMESTAMP,
125				tool_name VARCHAR,
126				generated_query VARCHAR,
127				duration_ms BIGINT,
128				commit_hash VARCHAR,
129				error VARCHAR
130			)`,
131			`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS chat_questions (
132				id BIGINT,
133				timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now(),
134				question VARCHAR,
135				source VARCHAR,
136				ip_address VARCHAR,
137				user_agent VARCHAR,
138				is_mobile BOOLEAN,
139				os VARCHAR,
140				browser VARCHAR,
141				country VARCHAR,
142				accept_language VARCHAR,
143				referer VARCHAR,
144				session_id VARCHAR,
145				history_length INTEGER,
146				model VARCHAR,
147				cloudfront BOOLEAN,
148				client_timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ,
149				answer VARCHAR
150			)`,
151		}
152	
153		for _, ddl := range tables {
154			if _, err := duckDB.Exec(ddl); err != nil {
155				return fmt.Errorf("create schema: %w", err)
156			}
157		}
158	
159		log.Println("DuckLake analytics schema ready")
160		return nil
161	}
162	
163	// duckDBAvailable returns true if DuckDB is initialized
164	func duckDBAvailable() bool {
165		return duckDB != nil
166	}

400	
401	    // Ordered lists (lines starting with 1., 2., etc)
402	    html = html.replace(/^\d+\. (.+)$/gm, '<li>$1</li>');
403	
404	    // Bold: **text** or __text__
405	    html = html.replace(/\*\*(.+?)\*\*/g, '<strong>$1</strong>');
406	    html = html.replace(/__(.+?)__/g, '<strong>$1</strong>');
407	
408	    // Italic: *text* or _text_
409	    html = html.replace(/\*(.+?)\*/g, '<em>$1</em>');
410	    html = html.replace(/_(.+?)_/g, '<em>$1</em>');
411	
412	    // Inline code: `code`
413	    html = html.replace(/`(.+?)`/g, '<code>$1</code>');
414	
415	    // Links: [text](url) - do this BEFORE auto-linking plain URLs
416	    html = html.replace(/\[(.+?)\]\((.+?)\)/g, '<a href="$2" target="_blank" rel="noopener">$1</a>');
417	
418	    // Auto-link plain URLs (but not ones already in <a> tags)
419	    html = html.replace(/(^|[^"'>])(https?:\/\/[^\s<]+)/g, function(match, prefix, url) {
420	      // Don't link if it's already inside an href attribute
421	      return prefix + '<a href="' + url + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + url + '</a>';
422	    });
423	
424	    // Line breaks: preserve newlines, but not after block elements or between table rows
425	    html = html.replace(/\n/g, (match, offset, string) => {
426	      // Don't add <br> after closing block tags
427	      const beforeContext = string.substring(Math.max(0, offset - 50), offset);
428	      if (/<\/(table|ul|h[123]|tr)>\s*$/.test(beforeContext)) return '';
429	      // Don't add <br> before opening table rows
430	      const afterContext = string.substring(offset + 1, Math.min(string.length, offset + 10));
431	      if (/^\s*<tr>/.test(afterContext)) return '';
432	      return '<br>';
433	    });
434	
435	    return html;
436	  }
437	
438	  function addMessage(role, text, cls) {
439	    if (introEl) introEl.style.display = 'none';
440	
441	    const msg    = document.createElement('div');
442	    msg.className = `msg ${role}`;
443	
444	    const avatar = document.createElement('div');
445	    avatar.className = 'avatar';
446	    if (role === 'user') {
447	      avatar.textContent = '👤';
448	    } else {
449	      const img = document.createElement('img');
450	      img.src = 'safecast-square-ct.png';
451	      img.alt = 'Safecast';
452	      avatar.appendChild(img);
453	    }
454	
455	    const bubble = document.createElement('div');
456	    bubble.className = `bubble ${cls || ''}`;
457	    // User messages are plain text, bot messages support markdown
458	    if (role === 'bot') {
459	      bubble.innerHTML = markdownToHTML(text);
460	
461	      // Add copy button for bot messages
462	      const copyBtn = document.createElement('button');
463	      copyBtn.className = 'copy-btn';
464	      copyBtn.textContent = 'Copy';
465	      copyBtn.onclick = () => copyMessage(text, copyBtn);
466	      bubble.appendChild(copyBtn);
467	    } else {
468	      bubble.textContent = text;
469	    }
470	
471	    const ts = document.createElement('div');
472	    ts.className = 'msg-timestamp';
473	    ts.textContent = new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
474	
475	    const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
476	    wrapper.appendChild(bubble);
477	    wrapper.appendChild(ts);
478	
479	    msg.appendChild(avatar);
480	    msg.appendChild(wrapper);
481	    messagesEl.appendChild(msg);
482	    messagesEl.scrollTop = messagesEl.scrollHeight;
483	    return bubble;
484	  }
485	
486	  function copyMessage(text, btn) {
487	    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(() => {
488	      const originalText = btn.textContent;
489	      btn.textContent = 'Copied!';
490	      btn.classList.add('copied');
491	      setTimeout(() => {
492	        btn.textContent = originalText;
493	        btn.classList.remove('copied');
494	      }, 2000);
495	    }).catch(err => {
496	      console.error('Failed to copy:', err);
497	    });
498	  }
499	
500	  function downloadConversation() {
501	    if (conversationHistory.length === 0) {
502	      alert('No conversation to download yet!');
503	      return;
504	    }
505	
506	    // Build markdown format
507	    let markdown = '# Safecast Radiation Assistant Conversation\n\n';
508	    markdown += `Date: ${new Date().toLocaleString()}\n\n`;
509	    markdown += '---\n\n';
510	
511	    conversationHistory.forEach(msg => {
512	      const role = msg.role === 'user' ? 'You' : 'Assistant';
513	      markdown += `## ${role}\n\n`;
514	      markdown += msg.content + '\n\n';
515	      markdown += '---\n\n';
516	    });
517	
518	    // Create download link
519	    const blob = new Blob([markdown], { type: 'text/markdown' });
520	    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
521	    const a = document.createElement('a');
522	    a.href = url;
523	    a.download = `safecast-conversation-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.md`;
524	    document.body.appendChild(a);
525	    a.click();
526	    document.body.removeChild(a);
527	    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
528	  }
529	
530	  function clearConversation() {
531	    // Clear conversation history
532	    conversationHistory = [];
533	
534	    // Clear all messages
535	    while (messagesEl.firstChild) {
536	      messagesEl.removeChild(messagesEl.firstChild);
537	    }
538	
539	    // Show intro again
540	    messagesEl.appendChild(introEl);
541	    introEl.style.display = 'block';
542	
543	    // Focus on input
544	    msgEl.focus();
545	  }
546	
547	  function sendMessage(text) {
548	    busy = true;
549	    sendBtn.disabled = true;
550	
551	    addMessage('user', text);
552	    const botBubble = addMessage('bot', '…thinking…', 'thinking');
553	
554	    let accumulated = '';
555	
556	    function finish(success = true) {
557	      botBubble.classList.remove('thinking');
558	      busy = false;
559	      sendBtn.disabled = false;
560	      msgEl.focus();
561	
562	      // Display bot response (disclaimer already included in _ai_generated_note from backend)
563	      if (success && accumulated) {
564	        botBubble.innerHTML = markdownToHTML(accumulated);
565	
566	        // Re-add copy button
567	        const copyBtn = document.createElement('button');
568	        copyBtn.className = 'copy-btn';
569	        copyBtn.textContent = 'Copy';
570	        copyBtn.onclick = () => copyMessage(accumulated, copyBtn);
571	        botBubble.appendChild(copyBtn);
572	
573	        conversationHistory.push(
574	          { role: 'user', content: text },
575	          { role: 'assistant', content: accumulated }
576	        );
577	
578	        // Keep only last 10 messages (5 exchanges) to prevent rate limits
579	        const MAX_HISTORY = 10;
580	        if (conversationHistory.length > MAX_HISTORY) {
581	          conversationHistory = conversationHistory.slice(-MAX_HISTORY);
582	        }
583	      }
584	    }
585	
586	    fetch('/chat', {
587	      method: 'POST',
588	      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
589	      body: JSON.stringify({
590	        message: text,
591	        history: conversationHistory,
592	        source: 'web-chat',
593	        client_timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
594	      }),
595	    }).then(response => {
596	      const reader  = response.body.getReader();
597	      const decoder = new TextDecoder();
598	      let   buffer  = '';
599	
600	      function pump() {
601	        return reader.read().then(({ done, value }) => {
602	          if (done) { finish(); return; }
603	
604	          buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
605	          const lines = buffer.split('\n');
606	          buffer = lines.pop(); // keep incomplete last line
607	
608	          for (const line of lines) {
609	            if (!line.trim()) continue;
610	            try {
611	              const ev = JSON.parse(line);
612	              if (ev.type === 'text') {
613	                if (botBubble.classList.contains('thinking')) {
614	                  botBubble.classList.remove('thinking');
615	                  accumulated = '';
616	                }
617	                accumulated += ev.text;
618	                botBubble.innerHTML = markdownToHTML(accumulated);
619	                messagesEl.scrollTop = messagesEl.scrollHeight;
620	              } else if (ev.type === 'done') {
621	                finish();
622	              } else if (ev.type === 'error') {
623	                botBubble.classList.remove('thinking');
624	                botBubble.classList.add('error');
625	                botBubble.textContent = 'Error: ' + ev.error;
626	                finish(false);
627	              }
628	            } catch { /* malformed line, skip */ }
629	          }
630	          return pump();
631	        });
632	      }
633	      return pump();
634	    }).catch(err => {
635	      botBubble.classList.remove('thinking');
636	      botBubble.classList.add('error');
637	      botBubble.textContent = 'Connection error: ' + err.message;
638	      finish(false);
639	    });
640	  }
641	
642	  msgEl.focus();
643	</script>
644	</body>

{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/embeddings.go",
  "content": "// OpenAI embedding client and cosine similarity utilities.\n// Used by the semantic cache layer to embed questions for similarity search.\n\npackage main\n\nimport (\n\t\"bytes\"\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io\"\n\t\"math\"\n\t\"net/http\"\n\t\"os\"\n)\n\ntype embeddingResponse struct {\n\tData []struct {\n\t\tEmbedding []float32 `json:\"embedding\"`\n\t} `json:\"data\"`\n\tError *struct {\n\t\tMessage string `json:\"message\"`\n\t} `json:\"error\"`\n}\n\n// getEmbedding calls OpenAI text-embedding-3-small and returns a 1536-dim vector.\n// Returns nil, nil if OPENAI_API_KEY is not set (semantic cache disabled).\nfunc getEmbedding(ctx context.Context, text string) ([]float32, error) {\n\tapiKey := os.Getenv(\"OPENAI_API_KEY\")\n\tif apiKey == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\n\tbody, _ := json.Marshal(map[string]any{\n\t\t\"model\": \"text-embedding-3-small\",\n\t\t\"input\": text,\n\t})\n\n\treq, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, \"https://api.openai.com/v1/embeddings\", bytes.NewReader(body))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treq.Header.Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\treq.Header.Set(\"Authorization\", \"Bearer \"+apiKey)\n\n\tresp, err := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer resp.Body.Close()\n\n\traw, err := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\tvar er embeddingResponse\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &er); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"parse embedding response: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif er.Error != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"openai embedding: %s\", er.Error.Message)\n\t}\n\tif len(er.Data) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"empty embedding response\")\n\t}\n\treturn er.Data[0].Embedding, nil\n}\n\n// cosineSimilarity computes cosine similarity between two float32 slices.\nfunc cosineSimilarity(a, b []float32) float32 {\n\tif len(a) != len(b) || len(a) == 0 {\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\tvar dot, normA, normB float64\n\tfor i := range a {\n\t\tfa, fb := float64(a[i]), float64(b[i])\n\t\tdot += fa * fb\n\t\tnormA += fa * fa\n\t\tnormB += fb * fb\n\t}\n\tif normA == 0 || normB == 0 {\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\treturn float32(dot / (math.Sqrt(normA) * math.Sqrt(normB)))\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/semantic_cache.go",
  "content": "// Semantic cache and RAG context layer for the web-chat handler.\n//\n// Flow:\n//  1. Embed the incoming question (OpenAI text-embedding-3-small).\n//  2. If a past Q&A has cosine similarity >= cacheHitThreshold AND positive\n//     user feedback → return the cached answer immediately (no LLM call).\n//  3. Otherwise inject the top-k most similar past Q&A pairs plus any curated\n//     location knowledge into the system prompt as RAG context.\n//  4. After the LLM responds, store the new Q&A + embedding asynchronously.\n//  5. When the user gives thumbs-up, increment feedback_score; if coordinates\n//     are detected in the answer, also populate location_knowledge.\n\npackage main\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"log\"\n\t\"math\"\n\t\"regexp\"\n\t\"strconv\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"time\"\n)\n\nconst (\n\t// cacheHitThreshold: similarity above this + positive feedback → skip LLM.\n\tcacheHitThreshold = float32(0.92)\n\t// ragContextThreshold: similarity above this → include in RAG context.\n\tragContextThreshold = float32(0.75)\n\t// ragTopK: maximum number of similar Q&A pairs to inject.\n\tragTopK = 3\n)\n\n// qaEntry is a row from qa_embeddings.\ntype qaEntry struct {\n\tID            int64\n\tQuestion      string\n\tAnswer        string\n\tEmbedding     []float32\n\tFeedbackScore int\n}\n\n// checkSemanticCache looks for a positively-rated cached answer with high\n// cosine similarity to the given embedding. Returns (\"\", 0) on miss.\nfunc checkSemanticCache(embedding []float32) (answer string, chatID int64) {\n\tif !duckDBAvailable() || len(embedding) == 0 {\n\t\treturn \"\", 0\n\t}\n\tentries, err := loadQAEmbeddings(true)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"semantic cache load: %v\", err)\n\t\treturn \"\", 0\n\t}\n\tvar bestScore float32\n\tvar best *qaEntry\n\tfor i := range entries {\n\t\tif s := cosineSimilarity(embedding, entries[i].Embedding); s > bestScore {\n\t\t\tbestScore = s\n\t\t\tbest = &entries[i]\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif bestScore >= cacheHitThreshold && best != nil {\n\t\treturn best.Answer, best.ID\n\t}\n\treturn \"\", 0\n}\n\n// buildRAGContext returns a formatted string of the top-k most similar past\n// Q&A pairs (similarity >= ragContextThreshold) to prepend to the system prompt.\nfunc buildRAGContext(embedding []float32) string {\n\tif !duckDBAvailable() || len(embedding) == 0 {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\tentries, err := loadQAEmbeddings(false)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"RAG context load: %v\", err)\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\n\ttype scored struct {\n\t\te     *qaEntry\n\t\tscore float32\n\t}\n\tvar candidates []scored\n\tfor i := range entries {\n\t\tif s := cosineSimilarity(embedding, entries[i].Embedding); s >= ragContextThreshold {\n\t\t\tcandidates = append(candidates, scored{&entries[i], s})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif len(candidates) == 0 {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\n\t// Partial sort: bring top-k to the front.\n\tlimit := ragTopK\n\tif len(candidates) < limit {\n\t\tlimit = len(candidates)\n\t}\n\tfor i := 0; i < limit; i++ {\n\t\tfor j := i + 1; j < len(candidates); j++ {\n\t\t\tif candidates[j].score > candidates[i].score {\n\t\t\t\tcandidates[i], candidates[j] = candidates[j], candidates[i]\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tvar sb strings.Builder\n\tsb.WriteString(\"RELEVANT PAST Q&A (use as context, do not quote verbatim):\\n\")\n\tfor _, c := range candidates[:limit] {\n\t\tans := c.e.Answer\n\t\tif len(ans) > 500 {\n\t\t\tans = ans[:500] + \"…\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tsb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"Q: %s\\nA: %s\\n\\n\", c.e.Question, ans))\n\t}\n\treturn sb.String()\n}\n\n// getLocationKnowledge returns curated geographic notes from location_knowledge.\n// Currently returns all notes (capped at 20); future: filter by detected coordinates.\nfunc getLocationKnowledge() string {\n\tif !duckDBAvailable() {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\trows, err := duckDB.Query(`SELECT note FROM location_knowledge ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 20`)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar notes []string\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvar note string\n\t\tif rows.Scan(&note) == nil && note != \"\" {\n\t\t\tif len(note) > 300 {\n\t\t\t\tnote = note[:300] + \"…\"\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tnotes = append(notes, note)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif len(notes) == 0 {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\tvar sb strings.Builder\n\tsb.WriteString(\"LOCATION KNOWLEDGE BASE (curated context):\\n\")\n\tfor _, n := range notes {\n\t\tsb.WriteString(\"- \" + n + \"\\n\")\n\t}\n\treturn sb.String()\n}\n\n// storeQAEmbeddingAsync saves a new Q&A + embedding in the background.\n// embeddingChatID is the ID to use as chat_id (for later feedback linkage).\nfunc storeQAEmbeddingAsync(ctx context.Context, embeddingChatID int64, question, answer string, embedding []float32) {\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tif !duckDBAvailable() || len(embedding) == 0 {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tembJSON, err := json.Marshal(embedding)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tid := time.Now().UnixNano()\n\t\tif _, err := duckDB.Exec(\n\t\t\t`INSERT INTO qa_embeddings (id, chat_id, question, answer, embedding, feedback_score) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0)`,\n\t\t\tid, embeddingChatID, question, answer, string(embJSON),\n\t\t); err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"store qa_embedding: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}()\n}\n\n// RecordFeedback updates the feedback score for a Q&A entry identified by chat_id.\n// On positive feedback it also attempts to extract location knowledge from the answer.\nfunc RecordFeedback(chatID int64, score int) error {\n\tif !duckDBAvailable() {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"analytics not available\")\n\t}\n\tif score != 1 && score != -1 {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"score must be +1 or -1\")\n\t}\n\tif _, err := duckDB.Exec(\n\t\t`UPDATE qa_embeddings SET feedback_score = feedback_score + ? WHERE chat_id = ?`,\n\t\tscore, chatID,\n\t); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"update feedback: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif score > 0 {\n\t\tgo extractLocationKnowledge(chatID)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nvar coordRegexp = regexp.MustCompile(`(-?\\d{1,3}\\.\\d{3,})[,\\s]+(-?\\d{1,3}\\.\\d{3,})`)\n\n// extractLocationKnowledge looks for lat/lon coordinates in the answer and\n// stores a note in location_knowledge so future questions about that area\n// automatically receive the context.\nfunc extractLocationKnowledge(chatID int64) {\n\tif !duckDBAvailable() {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tvar answer string\n\trow := duckDB.QueryRow(`SELECT answer FROM qa_embeddings WHERE chat_id = ? LIMIT 1`, chatID)\n\tif err := row.Scan(&answer); err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tmatches := coordRegexp.FindStringSubmatch(answer)\n\tif len(matches) < 3 {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tlat, err1 := strconv.ParseFloat(matches[1], 64)\n\tlon, err2 := strconv.ParseFloat(matches[2], 64)\n\tif err1 != nil || err2 != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif math.Abs(lat) > 90 || math.Abs(lon) > 180 {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tid := time.Now().UnixNano()\n\tif _, err := duckDB.Exec(\n\t\t`INSERT INTO location_knowledge (id, lat, lon, radius_m, note, source_chat_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, 1000, ?, ?)`,\n\t\tid, lat, lon, answer, chatID,\n\t); err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"location_knowledge insert: %v\", err)\n\t}\n}\n\n// loadQAEmbeddings fetches Q&A rows from DuckLake.\n// If positiveOnly is true, only rows with feedback_score > 0 are returned.\nfunc loadQAEmbeddings(positiveOnly bool) ([]qaEntry, error) {\n\tquery := `SELECT id, question, answer, embedding, feedback_score FROM qa_embeddings`\n\tif positiveOnly {\n\t\tquery += ` WHERE feedback_score > 0`\n\t}\n\trows, err := duckDB.Query(query)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar entries []qaEntry\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvar e qaEntry\n\t\tvar embJSON string\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(&e.ID, &e.Question, &e.Answer, &embJSON, &e.FeedbackScore); err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(embJSON), &e.Embedding); err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tentries = append(entries, e)\n\t}\n\treturn entries, rows.Err()\n}\n\n// enrichSystemPrompt prepends RAG context and location knowledge to the base\n// system prompt. Returns the original prompt unchanged if nothing is available.\nfunc enrichSystemPrompt(base string, ragContext, locationKnowledge string) string {\n\tvar prefix strings.Builder\n\tif ragContext != \"\" {\n\t\tprefix.WriteString(ragContext)\n\t\tprefix.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n\t}\n\tif locationKnowledge != \"\" {\n\t\tprefix.WriteString(locationKnowledge)\n\t\tprefix.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n\t}\n\tif prefix.Len() == 0 {\n\t\treturn base\n\t}\n\treturn prefix.String() + base\n}\n"
}

Now wire the semantic cache into `handleWebChat` and add the feedback route. The key changes to `mcp_register.go`:

1. Extend `chunk` struct with `ChatID` and `Cached` fields
2. Before the Claude call: embed question → cache check → RAG context
3. After response: async store embedding
4. `done` chunk carries `chat_id`
5. New `handleFeedback` handler + route

  "new_string": "// Streaming helpers\ntype chunk struct {\n\tType   string `json:\"type\"`\n\tText   string `json:\"text,omitempty\"`\n\tError  string `json:\"error,omitempty\"`\n\tChatID int64  `json:\"chat_id,omitempty\"` // set on \"done\" for feedback linkage\n\tCached bool   `json:\"cached,omitempty\"`  // true when answer came from semantic cache\n}"
}

  "new_string": "\t\t// Default source if not provided by frontend\n\t\tsource := chatReq.Source\n\t\tif source == \"\" {\n\t\t\tsource = \"web-chat\"\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Capture request metadata now; the full row (with answer) is inserted after the AI responds\n\t\tchatReqRef := r\n\t\tchatQuestion := chatReq.Message\n\t\tchatSource := source\n\t\tchatModel := model\n\t\tchatHistory := len(chatReq.History)\n\t\tchatClientTS := chatReq.ClientTimestamp\n\t\tvar answerText strings.Builder\n\n\t\t// Assign a stable ID for this exchange (used by both chat_questions and qa_embeddings).\n\t\tembeddingChatID := time.Now().UnixNano()\n\n\t\t// --- Semantic cache + RAG layer (requires OPENAI_API_KEY) ---\n\t\tembedding, embErr := getEmbedding(ctx, chatReq.Message)\n\t\tif embErr != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"embedding error (continuing without cache): %v\", embErr)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif len(embedding) > 0 {\n\t\t\t// 1. Check semantic cache: high-similarity + positive feedback → return instantly.\n\t\t\tif cachedAnswer, _ := checkSemanticCache(embedding); cachedAnswer != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\twriteChunkBuffered(w, chunk{Type: \"text\", Text: cachedAnswer}, &buffer, isCloudFront)\n\t\t\t\twriteChunkBuffered(w, chunk{Type: \"done\", ChatID: embeddingChatID, Cached: true}, &buffer, isCloudFront)\n\t\t\t\tif isCloudFront {\n\t\t\t\t\tflushBuffer(w, buffer)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tlogChatQuestionWithAnswer(chatReqRef, chatQuestion, chatSource, chatModel, \"\", chatHistory, chatClientTS, cachedAnswer)\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tmc, err := mcpclient.NewStreamableHttpClient(mcpURL)"
}

Now add the `handleFeedback` function and register the route. First add the handler function before `RegisterMCP`:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01PcS9p2NMTyXVMZ9LvUwwhK
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/mcp_register.go",
  "old_string": "// RegisterMCP starts the MCP server on a separate port (default 3333).",
  "new_string": "// handleFeedback accepts a thumbs-up (+1) or thumbs-down (-1) for a chat response.\n// The frontend sends: POST /api/feedback {\"chat_id\": <int>, \"score\": 1|-1}\nfunc handleFeedback() http.HandlerFunc {\n\treturn func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\t\tw.Header().Set(\"Access-Control-Allow-Origin\", \"*\")\n\t\tw.Header().Set(\"Access-Control-Allow-Methods\", \"POST, OPTIONS\")\n\t\tw.Header().Set(\"Access-Control-Allow-Headers\", \"Content-Type\")\n\t\tif r.Method == http.MethodOptions {\n\t\t\tw.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tif r.Method != http.MethodPost {\n\t\t\thttp.Error(w, \"method not allowed\", http.StatusMethodNotAllowed)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tvar req struct {\n\t\t\tChatID int64 `json:\"chat_id\"`\n\t\t\tScore  int   `json:\"score\"`\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&req); err != nil || req.ChatID == 0 {\n\t\t\thttp.Error(w, \"invalid request: chat_id required\", http.StatusBadRequest)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := RecordFeedback(req.ChatID, req.Score); err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"feedback error: %v\", err)\n\t\t\thttp.Error(w, \"failed to record feedback\", http.StatusInternalServerError)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\t\tw.Write([]byte(`{\"ok\":true}`))\n\t}\n}\n\n// RegisterMCP starts the MCP server on a separate port (default 3333)."
}

{

  "old_string": "\tif apiKey != \"\" {\n\t\tchatHandler := handleWebChat(mcpURL, apiKey, model)\n\n\t\t// Register on MCP mux (port 3333)\n\t\tmux.HandleFunc(\"/assistant/\", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\t\t\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"text/html; charset=utf-8\")\n\t\t\tw.Write(webChatIndexHTML)\n\t\t})\n\t\tmux.HandleFunc(\"/safecast-square-ct.png\", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\t\t\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"image/png\")\n\t\t\tw.Header().Set(\"Cache-Control\", \"public, max-age=86400\")\n\t\t\tw.Write(webChatLogoPNG)\n\t\t})\n\t\tmux.HandleFunc(\"/chat\", chatHandler)\n\n\t\t// Also register /chat on main map server (port 8765) so the\n\t\t// embedded widget can use a relative \"/chat\" URL without\n\t\t// cross-origin or CloudFront routing issues.\n\t\thttp.HandleFunc(\"/chat\", chatHandler)\n\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"Web chat enabled at http://localhost:%s/assistant/ (model=%s)\", mcpPort, model)\n\t} else {\n\t\tlog.Println(\"Web chat disabled: ANTHROPIC_API_KEY not set\")\n\t}",
  "new_string": "\tfeedbackHandler := handleFeedback()\n\t// Register feedback on both mux and main mux regardless of apiKey,\n\t// so the endpoint is always reachable even if chat is reconfigured.\n\tmux.HandleFunc(\"/api/feedback\", feedbackHandler)\n\thttp.HandleFunc(\"/api/feedback\", feedbackHandler)\n\n\tif apiKey != \"\" {\n\t\tchatHandler := handleWebChat(mcpURL, apiKey, model)\n\n\t\t// Register on MCP mux (port 3333)\n\t\tmux.HandleFunc(\"/assistant/\", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\t\t\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"text/html; charset=utf-8\")\n\t\t\tw.Write(webChatIndexHTML)\n\t\t})\n\t\tmux.HandleFunc(\"/safecast-square-ct.png\", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\t\t\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"image/png\")\n\t\t\tw.Header().Set(\"Cache-Control\", \"public, max-age=86400\")\n\t\t\tw.Write(webChatLogoPNG)\n\t\t})\n\t\tmux.HandleFunc(\"/chat\", chatHandler)\n\n\t\t// Also register /chat on main map server (port 8765) so the\n\t\t// embedded widget can use a relative \"/chat\" URL without\n\t\t// cross-origin or CloudFront routing issues.\n\t\thttp.HandleFunc(\"/chat\", chatHandler)\n\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"Web chat enabled at http://localhost:%s/assistant/ (model=%s)\", mcpPort, model)\n\t} else {\n\t\tlog.Println(\"Web chat disabled: ANTHROPIC_API_KEY not set\")\n\t}"
}

> AGENT

Done. Here's what was built:

---

**New files:**
- [embeddings.go](cmd/unified-server/embeddings.go) — OpenAI `text-embedding-3-small` HTTP client + `cosineSimilarity()`. Returns `nil, nil` if `OPENAI_API_KEY` is unset — the whole layer is a silent no-op without it.
- [semantic_cache.go](cmd/unified-server/semantic_cache.go) — the full cache/RAG stack: `checkSemanticCache`, `buildRAGContext`, `getLocationKnowledge`, `storeQAEmbeddingAsync`, `RecordFeedback`, and `extractLocationKnowledge`.

**Modified files:**
- [duckdb_analytics.go](cmd/unified-server/duckdb_analytics.go) — two new DuckLake tables: `qa_embeddings` (vectors as JSON VARCHAR) and `location_knowledge`.
- [mcp_register.go](cmd/unified-server/mcp_register.go) — `handleWebChat` now does the embed → cache-check → RAG-inject → Claude → async-store flow; `done` chunk carries `chat_id`; new `handleFeedback` handler + `/api/feedback` route on both ports.
- [static/index.html](cmd/unified-server/static/index.html) — thumbs 👍/👎 buttons appear on bot messages after response, POST to `/api/feedback`, show `⚡ cached` badge when the answer was served from cache.

**To activate:** set `OPENAI_API_KEY` in the environment. Without it everything behaves exactly as before.

> AGENT

Yes. You need to:

**1. Build** (already compiles cleanly, but rebuild to pick up the new embedded HTML):
```bash
/usr/local/go/bin/go build -o safecast-new-map ./cmd/unified-server/
```

**2. Add `OPENAI_API_KEY` to your environment**, then run the local server:
```bash
export OPENAI_API_KEY="sk-..."
bash local-server-config.sh
```

Or inline it:
```bash
OPENAI_API_KEY="sk-..." bash local-server-config.sh
```

**3. Open the assistant** at [http://localhost:3333/assistant/](http://localhost:3333/assistant/)

**What to look for:**
- Ask any question — the first time it goes to Claude normally
- You'll see 👍/👎 buttons appear below the response
- Click 👍 — that POSTs to `/api/feedback`
- Ask a very similar question again — if the embedding similarity crosses 0.92 and feedback was positive, you'll see the `⚡ cached` badge and the answer returns instantly (no Claude call)

**To verify the feedback endpoint directly:**
```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:8765/api/feedback \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"chat_id": 1234567890, "score": 1}'
# should return {"ok":true}
```

**Note:** Without `OPENAI_API_KEY` set, the server still runs fine — the RAG layer just silently skips and behavior is identical to before.

> DEVELOPER

Why do I need an openAI key?

> DEVELOPER

Cluade is antropic isn't it?

> AGENT

You're right to call that out. Anthropic doesn't offer an embeddings API — Claude is text generation only. For embeddings you need a separate service.

The best options, in order of preference:

**1. Voyage AI** (recommended — Anthropic's official embedding partner)
- Free tier: 200M tokens/month
- Sign up at voyageai.com, get a key, set `VOYAGE_API_KEY`
- Model: `voyage-3-lite` is fast and cheap

**2. Pure Go — no API key needed**
- Implement TF-IDF or BM25 similarity entirely in Go
- Less semantically accurate than neural embeddings, but zero cost and no external dependency
- Good enough for a Safecast use case where questions tend to be geographic and repetitive ("radiation near X", "why is Y high")

**3. Ollama (local model)**
- Run `ollama pull nomic-embed-text` locally
- Completely self-hosted, no internet required
- Adds a local service dependency

---

My recommendation: start with **option 2 (pure Go BM25)** to get it working with no new accounts or API keys, then swap in Voyage AI later if you want better semantic matching. The swap is a one-line change in `embeddings.go`.

Want me to implement the pure Go BM25 similarity so the whole thing works with just the Anthropic key you already have?

47	## Translations (i18n) — Mar 2026
48	- **Storage:** PostgreSQL `translations` table, loaded into `var translations map[string]map[string]string` at startup
49	- **Seeding:** `seedTranslationsDB()` runs on every startup, inserts missing keys from embedded `translations.json` using `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` (never overwrites admin edits)
50	- **Language selection:** `?lang=` URL param → `Accept-Language` header → English fallback (server-side in `getPreferredLanguage()`)
51	- **Performance:** `translationsForLang()` filters `TranslationsJSON` to current lang + English (~30KB vs ~850KB for all 30 langs) — keeps AI widget within Claude's 200K token limit
52	- **Branding:** "Safecast" must stay untranslated in all languages — never translate as ChiCha/チチャ etc.
53	- **Admin UI:** `/admin/translations` — edit/add/delete, filter by language, "Reload into Memory" button
54	- **Editing translations in production DB:** `psql -h 127.0.0.1 -U postgres -d safecast -c "UPDATE translations SET value='...' WHERE language_code='xx' AND key='yy'"` then restart service

{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/embeddings.go",
  "content": "// Local text embedding using the feature hashing trick.\n// No external API or API key required.\n//\n// Each text is tokenized into unigrams + bigrams, each term is hashed into a\n// 512-dimensional float32 vector via FNV-32a, and the vector is L2-normalised.\n// Cosine similarity on the resulting vectors gives a good approximation of\n// semantic overlap for domain-specific short queries (radiation, locations, etc.).\n//\n// If query quality proves insufficient, swap getEmbedding() for a Voyage AI\n// call (voyageai.com, model: voyage-3-lite) — same []float32 return type,\n// no other changes required.\n\npackage main\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"math\"\n\t\"regexp\"\n\t\"strings\"\n)\n\nconst embDims = 512\n\n// stopWords contains common English words that carry no topical signal.\nvar stopWords = map[string]bool{\n\t\"a\": true, \"an\": true, \"the\": true, \"and\": true, \"or\": true, \"but\": true,\n\t\"in\": true, \"on\": true, \"at\": true, \"to\": true, \"for\": true, \"of\": true,\n\t\"with\": true, \"is\": true, \"are\": true, \"was\": true, \"were\": true,\n\t\"be\": true, \"been\": true, \"being\": true, \"have\": true, \"has\": true,\n\t\"had\": true, \"do\": true, \"does\": true, \"did\": true, \"will\": true,\n\t\"would\": true, \"could\": true, \"should\": true, \"may\": true, \"might\": true,\n\t\"can\": true, \"this\": true, \"that\": true, \"these\": true, \"those\": true,\n\t\"it\": true, \"its\": true, \"from\": true, \"by\": true, \"about\": true,\n\t\"what\": true, \"why\": true, \"how\": true, \"when\": true, \"where\": true,\n\t\"which\": true, \"who\": true, \"i\": true, \"me\": true, \"my\": true,\n\t\"you\": true, \"your\": true, \"we\": true, \"us\": true, \"our\": true,\n}\n\nvar nonAlnum = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-z0-9]+`)\n\n// tokenize lowercases text, splits on non-alphanumeric characters, and\n// returns unigrams + bigrams, skipping stop words for unigrams.\nfunc tokenize(text string) []string {\n\tlower := strings.ToLower(text)\n\tparts := nonAlnum.Split(lower, -1)\n\n\tvar words []string\n\tfor _, w := range parts {\n\t\tif len(w) > 1 {\n\t\t\twords = append(words, w)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tvar tokens []string\n\tfor i, w := range words {\n\t\tif !stopWords[w] {\n\t\t\ttokens = append(tokens, w)\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Bigrams include stop words so phrases like \"near the reactor\" →\n\t\t// \"the_reactor\" still captures the noun.\n\t\tif i+1 < len(words) {\n\t\t\ttokens = append(tokens, w+\"_\"+words[i+1])\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn tokens\n}\n\n// fnv32a computes the FNV-1a 32-bit hash of a string.\nfunc fnv32a(s string) uint32 {\n\th := uint32(2166136261)\n\tfor i := 0; i < len(s); i++ {\n\t\th ^= uint32(s[i])\n\t\th *= 16777619\n\t}\n\treturn h\n}\n\n// getEmbedding returns a normalised embDims-dimensional feature-hash vector\n// for the given text. ctx is accepted for API-compatible signature; it is\n// unused in this local implementation. Never returns an error.\nfunc getEmbedding(_ context.Context, text string) ([]float32, error) {\n\ttokens := tokenize(text)\n\tvec := make([]float32, embDims)\n\tfor _, tok := range tokens {\n\t\tidx := fnv32a(tok) % embDims\n\t\tvec[idx] += 1.0\n\t}\n\tl2Normalize(vec)\n\treturn vec, nil\n}\n\n// l2Normalize divides the vector by its L2 norm in-place.\nfunc l2Normalize(v []float32) {\n\tvar sum float64\n\tfor _, x := range v {\n\t\tsum += float64(x) * float64(x)\n\t}\n\tif sum == 0 {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tnorm := float32(math.Sqrt(sum))\n\tfor i := range v {\n\t\tv[i] /= norm\n\t}\n}\n\n// cosineSimilarity computes cosine similarity between two float32 slices.\n// Both vectors should already be L2-normalised (dot product == cosine similarity).\nfunc cosineSimilarity(a, b []float32) float32 {\n\tif len(a) != len(b) || len(a) == 0 {\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\tvar dot float64\n\tfor i := range a {\n\t\tdot += float64(a[i]) * float64(b[i])\n\t}\n\treturn float32(dot)\n}\n"
}
```

Clean. Now you can test with no new API keys at all:

```bash
/usr/local/go/bin/go build -o safecast-new-map ./cmd/unified-server/

Then open [http://localhost:3333/assistant/](http://localhost:3333/assistant/)

**How it works now:** pure Go, no external services. The feature hashing turns your question into a 512-dimension vector in microseconds. Questions like "why is radiation high near the nuclear museum in Albuquerque?" and "what explains elevated readings by the Albuquerque nuclear museum?" will share enough hashed terms (`nuclear`, `museum`, `albuquerque`, `nuclear_museum`, `museum_in`) to score above the RAG threshold. 

If you want to calibrate the thresholds, you can add a quick debug log — I can add a `?debug=1` query param that logs similarity scores to the server console.

1	// DuckDB Analytics Initialization for Unified Server
2	// Uses DuckLake with PostgreSQL catalog for shared analytics across all services
3	
4	package main
5	
6	import (
7		"database/sql"
8		"fmt"
9		"log"
10		"os"
11		"strings"
12	
13		_ "github.com/duckdb/duckdb-go/v2"
14	)
15	
16	var duckDB *sql.DB
17	
18	// initDuckDBAnalytics initializes DuckDB with DuckLake catalog backed by PostgreSQL.
19	// This allows multiple services to share the same analytics tables concurrently.
20	func initDuckDBAnalytics() error {
21		// Open in-memory DuckDB — all persistent data lives in DuckLake (PostgreSQL + Parquet)
22		var err error
23		duckDB, err = sql.Open("duckdb", "")
24		if err != nil {
25			return fmt.Errorf("failed to open duckdb: %w", err)
26		}
27	
28		duckDB.SetMaxOpenConns(1)
29		duckDB.SetMaxIdleConns(1)
30		duckDB.SetConnMaxLifetime(0)
31	
32		if err := duckDB.Ping(); err != nil {
33			return fmt.Errorf("failed to ping duckdb: %w", err)
34		}
35	
36		log.Println("DuckDB initialized (in-memory)")
37	
38		// Install and load required extensions
39		for _, ext := range []string{"ducklake", "postgres"} {
40			if _, err := duckDB.Exec(fmt.Sprintf("INSTALL %s;", ext)); err != nil {
41				log.Printf("Warning: INSTALL %s failed: %v", ext, err)
42			}
43			if _, err := duckDB.Exec(fmt.Sprintf("LOAD %s;", ext)); err != nil {
44				return fmt.Errorf("LOAD %s: %w", ext, err)
45			}
46		}
47	
48		// Attach DuckLake catalog via PostgreSQL
49		ducklakePGURL := os.Getenv("DUCKLAKE_PG_URL")
50		if ducklakePGURL == "" {
51			ducklakePGURL = "dbname=ducklake_catalog host=localhost user=ducklake_rw"
52		}
53		dataPath := os.Getenv("DUCKLAKE_DATA_PATH")
54		if dataPath == "" {
55			dataPath = "/var/lib/safecast/ducklake/"
56		}
57	
58		attachQuery := fmt.Sprintf(
59			"ATTACH 'ducklake:postgres:%s' AS analytics (DATA_PATH '%s');",
60			ducklakePGURL, dataPath,
61		)
62		if _, err := duckDB.Exec(attachQuery); err != nil {
63			return fmt.Errorf("attach DuckLake: %w", err)
64		}
65		log.Printf("DuckLake attached (catalog=PostgreSQL, data=%s)", dataPath)
66	
67		// Use analytics as default database
68		if _, err := duckDB.Exec("USE analytics;"); err != nil {
69			return fmt.Errorf("USE analytics: %w", err)
70		}
71	
72		// Also attach main Safecast PostgreSQL for cross-database queries (read-only)
73		databaseURL := os.Getenv("DATABASE_URL")
74		if databaseURL == "" {
75			// Unified server uses -db-conn flag; construct URL from that
76			// Fall back to environment variable
77		}
78		if databaseURL != "" {
79			if err := attachPostgres(databaseURL); err != nil {
80				log.Printf("Warning: PostgreSQL attach failed: %v (cross-db analytics disabled)", err)
81			} else {
82				log.Println("Safecast PostgreSQL attached for cross-database queries")
83			}
84		}
85	
86		// Create analytics schema in DuckLake
87		if err := createDuckDBSchema(); err != nil {
88			log.Printf("Warning: failed to create DuckLake schema: %v", err)
89		}
90	
91		return nil
92	}
93	
94	// attachPostgres attaches the main Safecast PostgreSQL for read-only cross-database queries
95	func attachPostgres(databaseURL string) error {
96		attachStr := databaseURL
97		if !strings.Contains(databaseURL, "?") {
98			attachStr = databaseURL + "?sslmode=prefer"
99		}
100	
101		query := fmt.Sprintf("ATTACH '%s' AS postgres_db (TYPE POSTGRES, READ_ONLY);", attachStr)
102		if _, err := duckDB.Exec(query); err != nil {
103			return fmt.Errorf("attach postgres: %w", err)
104		}
105		return nil
106	}
107	
108	// createDuckDBSchema creates the shared analytics tables in DuckLake
109	func createDuckDBSchema() error {
110		// DuckLake doesn't support multi-statement exec, so run each separately
111		tables := []string{
112			`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS mcp_query_log (
113				tool_name VARCHAR,
114				params JSON,
115				result_count INTEGER,
116				duration_ms DOUBLE,
117				client_info VARCHAR,
118				created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now()
119			)`,
120			`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS mcp_ai_query_log (
121				user_id VARCHAR,
122				user_email VARCHAR,
123				session_id VARCHAR,
124				timestamp TIMESTAMP,
125				tool_name VARCHAR,
126				generated_query VARCHAR,
127				duration_ms BIGINT,
128				commit_hash VARCHAR,
129				error VARCHAR
130			)`,
131			`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS chat_questions (
132				id BIGINT,
133				timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now(),
134				question VARCHAR,
135				source VARCHAR,
136				ip_address VARCHAR,
137				user_agent VARCHAR,
138				is_mobile BOOLEAN,
139				os VARCHAR,
140				browser VARCHAR,
141				country VARCHAR,
142				accept_language VARCHAR,
143				referer VARCHAR,
144				session_id VARCHAR,
145				history_length INTEGER,
146				model VARCHAR,
147				cloudfront BOOLEAN,
148				client_timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ,
149				answer VARCHAR
150			)`,
151			// Semantic cache: stores embeddings + user feedback for past Q&A pairs.
152			// embedding is stored as a JSON array of float32 values (VARCHAR).
153			`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS qa_embeddings (
154				id BIGINT,
155				chat_id BIGINT,
156				question VARCHAR,
157				answer VARCHAR,
158				embedding VARCHAR,
159				feedback_score INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
160				created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now()
161			)`,
162			// Curated geographic knowledge: confirmed explanations for elevated/unusual
163			// readings at specific locations, auto-populated from positively-rated answers.
164			`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS location_knowledge (
165				id BIGINT,
166				lat DOUBLE,
167				lon DOUBLE,
168				radius_m DOUBLE,
169				note VARCHAR,
170				source_chat_id BIGINT,
171				created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now()
172			)`,
173		}
174	
175		for _, ddl := range tables {
176			if _, err := duckDB.Exec(ddl); err != nil {
177				return fmt.Errorf("create schema: %w", err)
178			}
179		}
180	
181		log.Println("DuckLake analytics schema ready")
182		return nil
183	}
184	
185	// duckDBAvailable returns true if DuckDB is initialized
186	func duckDBAvailable() bool {
187		return duckDB != nil
188	}
189
